# V5-02-R15-A1: execution contract, part 1 (R15, first documentation round)

Status (2026-11-06): R15-A1 candidate with correctives C01, C02, C03 and C04 as evidence and records, pending independent review of C04. The D81 prober ran (`probes/PROBES.md`, `probes/mutants.tsv`) and the final-candidate logs exist (`green/checks.txt`, `green/sql-final.txt`, `green/linkcheck-c02.txt`, `green/redaction-c02.txt` and `green/audit-final.txt`), as stated in Final results (C03). The C02 review findings C02-R1 to C02-R7 each have a row in the C03 closure table. Post-C01 status of the acceptance criteria is in section (l2); the C03 final candidate, its figures and its acceptance table (met or not met) are in 'C03 coordinator sync' at the end of this record. The C03 review (request-changes, R1 to R8) is answered by the section 'V5-02-R15-A1-C04 outcome' at the end of this record, which also states which FIN logs were written after the last edit and which three planned logs were never written. Nothing committed.

## Dispatch (2026-11-01, coordinator)

Kind: docs. Fresh agents. No product build, no test run, no mutation probe and no Docker run: the round changes only
Markdown contracts, one line of the `CLAUDE.md` authority list and round records. RA01 is complete and committed.

### Objective

Write the first half of the new exact contract `docs/vernacular/execution-contract.md` (destination decided by E14 A,
[execution-design.md](../execution-design.md) line 562 and decisions table line 1389) so that R15-B and R15-C can be
implemented without reopening design. Part 1 holds, as exact contract text in the style of
[authoring-contract.md](../authoring-contract.md) (common rules, one section per clause, messages table, pointer
amendments, traceability, acceptance checklist, decisions table):

- EX-1 Format3: the complete normative format3 SQL (every table STRICT, the immutable-table triggers, CHECKs, the
  records codec check, `PRAGMA user_version=3`, `ledger_meta.format CHECK(format=3)`), the init cutover to format3 (Q13 A)
  and the refusal of format1 and format2 by execution verbs with `unsupported_ledger_version`; the cross-row invariants
  the native writer enforces and the reader verifies, including the stated check-request exception.
- EX-2 Request framing and replay: `request_v3` (record kind 4, codec 3), replay and conflict rules, native assembly of
  packet-bearing responses from the saved result plus the attachment record.
- EX-3 The execution commit protocol: the seven ordered steps, the closed precondition vocabulary, `fact_version`,
  the delta write of `role_state` with the locked before-state check (lost-update guard) and the one exception for the
  check completion commit.
- EX-4 Facts, derived state and the reconcile rules: states, drift, the exact precedence, prerequisite resolution from
  ledger facts, the added event types, event payload version 2, the closed transition reasons and what `explain` reports.
- EX-5 Role structure value `role_structure_v1`, generation lineage (takeover fences only), and the execution fingerprint
  recipe `work_exec_v1` at the level the state machine needs (its full field recipe is part 2).
- EX-6 Interpretation binding and its position after early replay.
- EX-7 The closed error and message tables: the nine new codes of E16 A with exits and meaning, the one new message
  `Execution resource limit exceeded`, and the closed one-message-per-site rule.
- EX-8 Execution bounds table.
- The run state machine (run states `active`, `released`, `interrupted`, `completed`, `fenced`; role states; every legal
  transition and its reason) and the reconcile rules, as exact clauses.

RA01 complete (HEAD `d536922b`, working tree clean). X08, X07 and X07-C02 approved. No code dependency.

### Scope boundary: what this round must NOT do

- No part 2 text. The verbs and their option grammar, services, the dispatch projection, the `dispatch_manifest_v1`
  manifest, the full `work_exec_v1` field recipe, provenance (`driver_provenance_v1`), the `history --event` selector, the
  orchestrator live form and the pointer amendments to `command-contract.md`, `render-contract.md` and
  `load-command-contract.md` belong to R15-A2 in the same file. Leave clearly marked placeholder headings for each (for
  example "Part 2 (R15-A2): verbs and services — not yet written") so R15-A2 fills them in place. Placeholders carry no
  normative text.
- No R16 to R19 clause: tables that only later rounds use (`checkpoints`, `reservations`, `evidence`, `completions`,
  `publications`, `verdicts`, `repairs`, `blockers` and their states) are part of the format3 SQL because format3 is
  one schema, but their semantics are labelled "reserved for R16" through "R18" and no behaviour is specified.
- No source, test, build, Lua or evidence-script change. No narrowing of any approved contract clause. No change to an
  existing diagnostic message byte (the external-profile message table is closed; the only additions are the E16 A codes
  and the one execution message, each flagged as an amendment).
- `CLAUDE.md` stays descriptive: the only edit is one list entry for the new contract in authority item 2 (approved by E14 A).
  `AGENTS.md` stays a symlink.
- Render stays database-free; no `--claim`, `--refresh` or `--since` on render (Q14 A).

### Operator choices assigned to this round (E1 A sub-choices)

E1 A (decisions table, line 1376) delegates two sub-choices to R15-A1. The design does not decide them as text, so they are
raised here with recommendations. Per the operator's standing rule of 2026-10-31 (accept recommended options in vernacular
halts, stop only for Q17, push or merge, or an option without a recommendation), both have a recommendation and the run
proceeds on it; the author records each in the decisions table as operator-decided (delegated 2026-10-31, option A) and the
reviewer lists them for operator confirmation at commit.

1. **Replace `--confirm-stopped` (verb `check-recover`, built in R17; the replacement is settled here).** The flag form cannot
   be required because flags cannot be required ([external-profile-contract.md](../external-profile-contract.md) lines 258 to
   259; design note at line 417), so native would have to refuse its absence as usage. Options: A, a required scalar option
   `--confirm-stopped` whose only accepted value is `true` (any other value is `usage`); B, a required scalar option
   `--stopped-run <run id>` that must equal `--run-id`; C, keep the flag and refuse absence as usage. Recommendation A: it
   keeps the spelling already in the design, is a normal required scalar under the separate-token grammar, and a required
   value makes a missing confirmation a parse error rather than a semantic refusal. Part 1 records the choice in its
   decisions table and states no verb grammar beyond that; R15-A2 and the R17 contract restate it in the verb table.
2. **`history --request-id` and execution results (the `ledger-contract.md` line 446 selector).** Options: A, `history
   --request-id` also returns execution results, tokens included for claim and takeover requests, exactly as `status
   --request-id` does (design R15-9, line 646, makes this the default "unless E1 excludes it"); B, `history --request-id`
   returns only the load request record and execution results are read only through `status --request-id`. Recommendation
   A: the secret guards against accidental reuse, not ledger readers (E11 A), `history` is already a ledger read, and one
   lookup behaviour across both verbs avoids a second token-exposure rule to review. This changes the existing `history`
   request lookup for format3 only; format1 and format2 behaviour is byte-unchanged. Part 1 states the rule in EX-2 and the
   amendment to the ledger contract's request lookup sentence.

Any other choice the author finds that is not contract-decided, E1 to E17 or Q-decided is a halt, not a coder decision.

### Acceptance criteria

1. `execution-contract.md` states EX-1 to EX-8, the run state machine and the reconcile rules as exact text, in the structure
   and style of `authoring-contract.md`, so R15-B can write the format3 schema, `request_v3` and the commit path, and R15-C
   can write `reconcile.lua`, without a design choice. The format3 SQL is complete and self-consistent: every table, column,
   type, CHECK, trigger, index and foreign key is written, and it is internally consistent with the cross-row invariants.
2. The contract states, for every transition of the role state machine and the run state machine, the before state, the
   cause, the after state and the closed reason, and gives the reconcile precedence as an ordered list with the v5 lines
   it implements.
3. Closed code and message tables: the nine EX-7 codes with exit and meaning, the single new message, existing messages reused
   where their meaning fits (named, not respelled), and an explicit statement that no other new code or message exists.
   Each new code is tagged as an E16 A amendment; XD4 is amended for execution only.
4. A traceability table maps every EX clause, the part 1 clauses of R15-1, R15-2, R15-3 and R15-9, each decision E1, E2, E3,
   E5, E11, E14, E16, rows A09, A10, A20, A22, A23, A26 and A33 and the v5 lines to contract sections. A decisions table marks
   every choice as contract-decided (citation), operator-decided (E or Q id) or new-engineering, and shows the two sub-choices
   above as operator-decided (delegated 2026-10-31, option A).
5. Part 2 placeholders exist, clearly marked, contain no normative text, and each names the R15-A2 clause list it will hold.
6. Pointer amendments are present, scoped and mutually consistent; `CLAUDE.md` gains exactly one list entry and nothing else;
   the `AGENTS.md` symlink is intact; no approved clause is narrowed and no existing diagnostic byte changes.
7. Every citation (file and line) was checked by reading the cited line; every relative link and anchor in every changed
   document resolves.
8. Redaction check exits 0 on each changed file and a positive control (a scratch file containing the repository root) exits
   non-zero.
9. No source byte changed: the workflow `identity.py` over `src` and `tests` shows the tracked diff and untracked set empty
   for those pathspecs.
10. Record rule (binding, new 2026-11-01): this record, its review and the evidence README do not pin sha256 values of other
    round records, review files, REPORT files or the roadmap. Hashes may pin only `src` and committed evidence data files.
    Records describe; they do not checksum each other.

### Agreed verification

1. A documentation author (Sonnet 5.5, medium) writes the contract and amendments. A search step produces
   `plan/DECISIONS.md` (Q18 A: contract-decided, designed, or new) and `plan/INVARIANTS.md` (one row per acceptance criterion
   naming the textual defect that would break it) under `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-evidence/plan/`; an Opus plan review checks
   them before authoring. The reviewer (Opus 5.5, high) then reviews the candidate against execution-design.md, the four
   cited contracts and v5, reading every cited line. No red check, gate runner build or mutation prober (no code).
2. Redaction: `python3 scripts/redact-local-paths.py --repo-path "$PWD" --home "$HOME" redact <file>` then `check <file>`
   with one file argument per invocation, for each changed file, exit 0; plus a positive control that exits non-zero.
3. Link and anchor check of every changed document (a small scratch script outside the checkout is acceptable; its output is
   preserved under the evidence folder).
4. `git diff --stat` shows only owned files; `AGENTS.md` is still a symlink to `CLAUDE.md`; the `src`/`tests` identity is empty.
5. On approval the coordinator applies records-only advisories, commits with explicit paths and `git commit -s` (no co-author
   trailer; the operator's rule overrides any attribution reminder), and updates the roadmap opening (R15-A1 approved) and
   Next work (R15-A2 next, then R15-B to R15-E). Nothing is pushed or merged.

| identity | value |
|---|---|

### (b) HALT-1: delegated decision under the 2026-10-31 standing delegation

HALT-1 (D54), its delegation under the standing delegation of 2026-10-31 (accept the recommended option in vernacular halts), asked which fixed text applies to the nine new E16 A codes and to the nine execution fallbacks. It is recorded as **delegated** under that standing delegation. Option A is applied: each new code gets one fixed message with no substitutions, and each execution operation other than load gets one fixed post-commit fallback. This is not a live operator answer. The operator may overrule it at commit review. The tag recorded for D54, D67 and D68 is exactly `operator-decided (delegated 2026-10-31, option A)`. The texts below are byte-exact from D67 and D68.

| Code | Message | Exit |
|---|---|---|
| `no_active_job` | `No active job matches the request` | 3 |
| `claim_conflict` | `Role state does not allow this operation` | 3 |
| `state_conflict` | `Execution state changed since the request was prepared` | 3 |
| `fenced` | `Run is not current` | 3 |
| `interpretation_mismatch` | `Interpretation does not match the active revision` | 3 |
| `history_unavailable` | `History needed for this request is unavailable` | 3 |
| `drifted` | `Role work changed since it was acknowledged` | 3 |
| `check_in_progress` | `A check is already in progress for this role` | 3 |
| `unsupported_policy` | `Execution policy is not supported` | 3 |
| `resource_limit` at new execution native sites (D53) | `Execution resource limit exceeded` | 3 |

Post-commit fallbacks (D68): for each of the nine execution operations `Activate`, `Claim`, `Takeover`, `Refresh`, `Checkpoint`, `Release`, `Check`, `Check-recover` and `Verdict`, the message is `<Operation> may be complete; retry the same request ID or inspect request history`, with code `io` and exit 4. The stdout-write fallback reuses the same nine strings. The count is 9 codes, 9 fallbacks and 1 resource message: 19 new messages (D54), the closed set.

## R15-A1 candidate sync (coordinator, 2026-11-01, appended before review; pre-C01, superseded by the C01 sections)

This part describes the R15-A1 candidate as it stood on 2026-11-01, before the corrective round V5-02-R15-A1-C01. It is NOT the final candidate: C01 changed `execution-contract.md` and `v5-alignment.md`, and the final identity, test results, probe results and invariant table are in the C01 sections below ('V5-02-R15-A1-C01 outcome and closure table'). Sections (a) to (f) above are the round's earlier additions; every figure there belongs to the V1 run or to the plan stage, and where it differs from this part, this part governed for the pre-C01 candidate. Nothing was repaired between the V1 run and this sync: the per-file hashes in section (g) equal the V1 per-file hashes. Every 'final', 'current' or 'live' label in sections (g) to (m) means 'the R15-A1 candidate before C01'.

### (k) Delegated decisions

Accepted at their recommended option under the operator's standing delegation of 2026-10-31. These are not live operator answers; the operator may overrule them at commit review.

- **HALT-1 (D54).** Question: what message text applies to the nine E16 A codes and to the execution post-commit fallbacks, given that E16 A authorises "the new public codes and the execution message" (execution-design.md:1391; XD4 at remaining-steps-design.md:67), the error envelope requires a message string (render-contract.md:613-614), no existing message fits `no_active_job`, `claim_conflict`, `state_conflict`, `fenced`, `interpretation_mismatch`, `history_unavailable`, `drifted`, `check_in_progress` or `unsupported_policy`, and EX-3 step 7 (execution-design.md:185-187) requires operation-specific fallback text in the closed message table. Option taken: A. Each new code has one fixed message with no substitutions; each execution operation has one fixed post-commit fallback modelled on the load text of load-command-contract.md:221; `Execution resource limit exceeded` stays as designed; format3 load keeps its existing fallback byte-exact. Every one is a flagged XD4 amendment for execution only, and I23 and acceptance 3 change from "one new message" to this enumerated closed set (9 codes, 9 fallbacks, 1 resource message). Rejected: B (reuse nearest existing text, which misleads), C (defer text to implementing rounds, which leaves a design choice open against acceptance 1 and Q18 A). Source: E16 A's stated reason (render-contract.md:626, drivers must not parse messages), the envelope's required message field (render-contract.md:613-614) and EX-3 step 7. The exact strings are in section (b) and `plan/DECISIONS.md` (D54, D67, D68). The tag recorded for D54, D67 and D68 is exactly `operator-decided (delegated 2026-10-31, option A)`.
- **OP1, OP2, OP3** (section (c)) are operator-decided (delegated 2026-10-31, option A) under the same standing rule; they are listed there, not repeated here. Their source is `plan/DECISIONS.md` section I.

Decision record: [plan/DECISIONS.md](../../../tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-evidence/plan/DECISIONS.md) (gitignored, in the working tree only). Its revision 4 is authoritative; superseded revisions are in `plan/superseded-1` to `-3`.

## Corrective dispatch V5-02-R15-A1-C01 (coordinator, 2026-11-01)

Kind: docs corrective. Fresh agents. Documentation, one repaired scratch checker and the invariant table only. No product
build, no source or test change.

### Objective

Close findings R1 to R12 of the [R15-A1 review](V5-02-R15-A1-review.md) on top of the uncommitted R15-A1 candidate. The
candidate is built upon, never reverted. Required outcomes:

- R1: the run token exposure list in the contract (EX-2 and the EX-4 token text) includes `status --request-id`, matching
  R15-9, R15-7, A09 and OP2. Invariant I54 is corrected to say so.
- R2: the event-level reason is the reason of the lowest transitions ordinal, which is ordinal 1, consistent with
  `CHECK(ordinal>0)`. Align convention C5, decision D43 and invariants I57 and I67.
- R3: OP1 option A is a required scalar, so the external-profile rule "Flags cannot be required" is unchanged. The
  contract says so and the text claiming it changes that rule is removed. Invariant I33 is aligned.
- R4: `role_state.run_id` is NULL and `drifted` is 0 for every non-running state, and `after_drifted` is 0 in the matching
  transitions case. Add the CHECKs to the format3 SQL and state how replay derives `run_id` from the transitions.
- R5: correct convention C6 and the `evidence.reason` text. The design (EX-1) says closed enum and R17-3 lists the values.
  If the values are fully listed, including the pass value, encode the set as a SQL CHECK now. Otherwise state
  "reader-verified only". Record the decision and its basis.
- R6: repair the scratch checker (SQL extraction must not stop at the first Reserved comment), correct I60 and I69 to agree
  with rows M8 and M10, fix I54 and I57, and re-run every check to zero FAIL. Run the D81 mutation prober over the final
  contract in a scratch copy outside the checkout (preferred), or withdraw D81 explicitly in the record with a reason.
- R7 to R12 (minor): header part 1 scope (EX-1 to EX-8 plus the state machines); the M5, M7 and M8 note (M7 and M8 include
  running and running with drift); D56 basis tag and D55 `internal` versus corruption wording; `v5-alignment.md` E2 and E5
  pointers (E2 points to EX-4, E5 is not about request framing); cutover wording "a new explicit target" per
  execution-design.md line 80; label the EX-8 check-output row as an R17 fact.

### Decisions (delegated, not live answers)

Under the standing delegation of 2026-10-31 these are accepted at their recommended options: OP1 option A (required scalar
`--confirm-stopped`, only value `true`), OP2 option A (`history --request-id` returns execution results like `status
--request-id`), OP3 option A (the `stale` to `done` output-vector restore row as recommended) and HALT-1 option A (D54, D67
and D68: nine fixed code messages, nine fixed post-commit fallbacks, one resource message, acceptance criterion 3 amended to
that enumerated set). Everywhere the contract, decisions table, invariant table and this record say "operator-pending,
recommended option applied", change it to "operator-decided (delegated 2026-10-31, option A)". Record them as delegated,
not as live answers; the operator may overrule them later. No halt: every other choice is decided by a cited source.

### Dependencies

R15-A1 candidate on HEAD `d536922b`. RA01 complete and committed. No other dependency.

### Owned files

Edit: `docs/vernacular/execution-contract.md`, `docs/vernacular/v5-alignment.md`, and the other R15-A1 candidate documents
only where a pending-status label or an amended sentence requires it (`docs/vernacular/ledger-contract.md`,

file. Evidence: `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-evidence/` (plan DECISIONS and INVARIANTS, the repaired scratch checker, green,
probes, REPORT.md and README.md). Raw logs are never deleted; superseded outputs move aside. `CLAUDE.md` is not edited again
(its authority entry is already in the candidate). The coordinator alone updates the roadmap opening and Next work after
approval. Every editor lists each file it edits in this record.

### Acceptance criteria

1. R1 to R12 are each closed with a cited location in the final contract.
2. The format3 SQL runs in `sqlite3` with foreign keys on; table, trigger and index counts are recorded; the new CHECKs
   (non-running role_state `run_id`/`drifted`, `after_drifted`, and the `evidence.reason` set if encoded) reject the bad
   rows in recorded scratch statements.

   D81 is withdrawn with a stated reason; survivors are listed.

   positive control that exits non-zero.

6. No approved clause is narrowed, no existing diagnostic byte changes, no existing check or test name is removed.
7. Record rule (binding): records and evidence do not pin sha256 values of other round records, review files, REPORT files or
   the roadmap. Hashes pin only `src` and committed evidence data files.
8. The delegated decisions are relabelled everywhere as in the Decisions section above.

### Decisions and amendments

- D97 to D101 are new-engineering rows of the contract Decisions table (execution-contract.md:1080 to 1084). D100 is the no-CHECK fallback: `evidence.reason` is native-writer enforced and reader-verified only, with no SQL CHECK, and R17-3 lists no `reason` value for a `pass` outcome, so R17 states that value. No stored format3 SQL changes.
- D102 (operator-decided, basis E11 A; R15-9; R15-7) is at execution-contract.md:1085. D103 (contract-decided) is at execution-contract.md:1086.
- D104 is plan and record only and is not in the contract. It decided D81 as RUN in the plan; the run did not happen (see D81 resolution below).
- Amended D43 (execution-contract.md:1044), D55 (execution-contract.md:1050) and D56 (execution-contract.md:1051).
- Relabelled D54 (execution-contract.md:1049), D67 (:1055), D68 (:1056), OP1 (:1087), OP2 (:1088) and OP3 (:1089) carry the exact tag `operator-decided (delegated 2026-10-31, option A)`. They are recorded as delegated, not as live answers, and the operator may overrule them. The earlier pending tag wording of these rows is gone from the contract, this record and the old plan files.

### Why the earlier logs are stale

File times, read with `stat` on 2026-11-06 (local time): `probes/baseline.txt` 12:12:36, `probes/baseline-closing.txt` 12:16:10, `probes/baseline-final.txt` 12:19:45, `green/checks-closing.txt` 12:21:17, `v5-alignment.md` 12:31:24 and `execution-contract.md` 13:33:18. Every prober baseline and every audit log was written before the last edits to the two repaired documents, and each of them reports pre-C01 wording (the old OP1 tag wording, no C01 closure section). This is an inference from file times and from the FAIL lines themselves, not a recorded cause: the logs do not state why the waves ran before the contract was finished. The consequence is firm: none of those logs describes the final candidate. They are kept, not edited. `green/sql-c01.txt` has the same property (it records 7 of 23 scratch statements differing from their expected result, all on pre-C01 SQL).

### D81 resolution

D81 resolution: RUN in C03 (the C01 text of this subsection is superseded). The mutation prober (D81, D104) ran over the final candidate in a scratch clone outside the checkout, with a runner that counts a mutant as killed only when the exit code is non-zero, a FAIL line names the row's check, that check is a PASS line of the baseline and the runner counts line satisfies P + F = R. The results are in `probes/PROBES.md`, `probes/mutants.tsv` and one log per mutant under `probes/raw/`. Main section totals: killed 95, survived 0, not run 0. Closing section (the audit rows, N9, N16, N17 and the C03 rows) totals: killed 66, survived 0, not run 0; all 161 verdicts are KILLED and each names its killing check. Three first verdicts were SURVIVED and were repaired in the checker (not the contract) and probed again: C58-m1 (CHK-I10-history-lookup), C60-m1 (CHK-I51-header) and C31-m12 (CHK-C01-prober-evidence); their first logs are kept as `probes/raw/*.survived-1.log` and the final verdict of each is KILLED (see Checker amendments (C03)). C55-m1 (an added `stale` to `ready` row M42) is killed by the row-count pin of CHK-I60-role-rows (FAIL message `M table row count != 41`), not by its stale-to-ready rule; that rule is exercised by C55-m2 and C55-m3. D81 is therefore resolved as RUN, with the counts above.

### Wave order

T1; then PA and PB; then R1, V1, PROBER, R2, PROBER2, FINAL, SYNC, PROBER3 and CLOSE (waves 1 to 11, `plan/DECISIONS.md` C.25 in the C01 folder). The file times in 'Why the earlier logs are stale' show that the prober and audit stages ran before PA and PB had finished.

### Scripts, record rule and limits

- Scripts run only from `<scratchpad>`. The evidence copies `red/ex_checks.py`, `green/sql_c01.py` and `green/linkcheck.py` were byte-identical to the scratchpad files when written (compared with `cmp`, no hash written), and no script is executed from `tests/native/`.
- Record rule: no sha256 of this record, the review file, any REPORT or the roadmap is written anywhere. Identity lines come only from the workflow `identity.py` run with pathspecs that exclude `docs/vernacular/rounds/` and `roadmap.md`, pasted verbatim.
- Limits: docs-only round; no product build, test binary or Docker gate; nothing is committed, pushed or merged. Open: the audit evidence files, the D81 prober (no survivor or masking reason can be stated), the redaction scope of `raw/`. Superseded in C03: the audit evidence files and the prober now exist (a survivor reason is in `probes/PROBES.md`); the C03 limits are in 'C03 remaining limits'. `--audit` also runs the core checks, and a second `red-audit-run.txt` run is not reproducible (see 'Repairs and amendments').

### Why RED.md was rewritten (C02)

The earlier `red/RED.md` described a `planar_tests` build and 69 `ctest` runs that each found no test, and it named every check not-registered. No CHK-* check is a ctest test and that attempt touched the build directory, so it was never red evidence, and it contradicted the real behavioural red in `red/red-run.txt` and `raw/red-checks`. C02 rewrote `red/RED.md` to give the command, the pre-fix identity lines and the failing check names of the real red, and to label the ctest logs an irrelevant first attempt, kept and not deleted.

### C02 red evidence

`red/red-c02-run.txt` and `red/red-c02-audit-run.txt` are the behavioural red of C02 on the pre-fix tree, that is the C01 candidate before any C02 edit: each holds the command (`ex_checks-c02.py --repo` with `--evidence-dir` and `--frozen-dir`, the audit run with `--audit`), the identity lines of the seven candidate documents and the empty `src tests` identity, and the FAIL lines on assertion messages. The default run has 12 FAIL lines (the C02 record, red and contract checks that the pre-fix tree cannot satisfy) and the audit run has 19; `CHK-C02-red-logs` checks both files against the expected FAIL sets.

## C01 review outcome (coordinator, 2026-11-06)

Review: [V5-02-R15-A1-C01-review.md](V5-02-R15-A1-C01-review.md). Decision: **request-changes, not records only**. The
reviewer found R1 to R12 of the R15-A1 review closed in the contract and alignment text (cited lines re-read, the format3

## Corrective dispatch V5-02-R15-A1-C02 (coordinator, 2026-11-06)

Kind: docs (evidence and records corrective). Fresh agents. This section is appended to the lineage record; C02 owns
no separate record file. No product build, no test run, no Docker run. Fresh agents for coding, mutation probing and
review.

### Objective

Close the eight findings of the [C01 review](V5-02-R15-A1-C01-review.md) (C01-R1 to C01-R8) without changing the
repaired contract text, except for one tag spelling. The reviewer accepted the contract: R1 to R5 and R7 to R12 of the
R15-A1 review are closed, the format3 SQL runs (24 STRICT tables, 43 triggers, 2 indexes, 25 of 25 accept and refuse
statements), links and redaction pass. What remains is evidence and records.

### Dependencies

HEAD `d536922b59bf9118b91e4188a26d770f9745d600`. The working tree is uncommitted and holds the R15-A1 plus C01
candidate; C02 builds on it and reverts none of it. No operator question is open: Q13 to Q17 are decided in the
[remaining-steps design](../remaining-steps-design.md), OP2 is delegated option A (2026-10-31), and a reasoned
withdrawal of D81 needs no operator decision, but running the prober is the chosen path.

### Pre-existing changes (do not touch beyond ownership)

Modified: `CLAUDE.md`, `acceptance-matrix.md`, `external-profile-contract.md`, `implementation.md`,
`ledger-contract.md`, `roadmap.md`, `v5-alignment.md`. Untracked: `execution-contract.md`, this record, the R15-A1
review and the C01 review. Evidence folders `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-evidence/` and `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c01-evidence/`
exist. No `src` or `tests` code byte differs from HEAD.

### Owned files

- `docs/vernacular/execution-contract.md`: only line 520, the OP2 tag (C01-R7).
- This record (`docs/vernacular/rounds/V5-02-R15-A1.md`): status line, section (c) wording, post-C01 acceptance status
  for criteria 1 to 10, D81 resolution, C02 outcome, staging list.
- `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c01-evidence/`: `probes/PROBES.md`, `probes/mutants.tsv`, `probes/raw/<id>.log`,
  `green/checks.txt`, `green/sql-final.txt`, a fresh audit log, a copy of the checker script used, `red/RED.md`
  (rewritten), and the coordinator's notes. No existing raw log is deleted or edited.
- Roadmap opening and Next work, only at commit time after approval.

### Work items

1. C01-R1 (blocking). Run the D81 mutation prober over the final contract in a scratch copy outside the checkout. For each
   guard row C5, C24 to C28, C34 to C38, C44 to C47, C49 to C56, C58 to C60, C59b and G1 to G8, apply its stated mutant
   to a scratch copy, run the repaired checker, and show it fails naming the check id. Write `probes/PROBES.md`,
   `mutants.tsv` and one `probes/raw/<id>.log` per mutant, with killed and survived counts. A survivor is a finding: fix
   the check (never the contract) and re-probe. Only the prober applies mutants; coders never mutate checkout files.
2. C01-R2 (blocking). Write final-candidate logs: `green/checks.txt` (repaired checker over the final contract, count and
   zero FAIL), `green/sql-final.txt` (sqlite3 with foreign keys on, the 25 accept and refuse statements and counts), and
   explain or fix the audit run that exits 1 with five FAIL lines (either the audit script is stale against corrected
   invariants: label and fix it; or it found real defects: report them). Copy the checker script used so the logs are
   reproducible. Include the identity block.
3. C01-R3. Rewrite `red/RED.md` to describe the real behavioural red (`red/red-run.txt`, `raw/red-checks`, command,
   pre-fix snapshot hash, failing check names) and label the ctest and not-registered logs as an irrelevant first attempt.
4. C01-R4 and R5. Correct the status line and add a post-C01 status for R15-A1 acceptance criteria 1 to 10 (criterion 3
   as amended by HALT-1).
5. C01-R6. Cite the reviewer's redaction positive control and run your own. The staging list must leave out, or redact
   under an explicit recorded decision, `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-evidence/inventory.txt` and
   `plan/superseded-*/INVARIANTS.md` (host path).
6. C01-R7. Use the exact tag `operator-decided (delegated 2026-10-31, option A)` at `execution-contract.md:520` and
   reword section (c) so it does not say the delegation "does not record an operator decision". The only contract edit.
7. C01-R8. State that the original checker run has 15 FAIL lines against 13 because I45 (diff scope) and I59 (record
   additions) ran on a tree that had grown since 2026-11-01, or correct the count.

### Binding rules

No existing check name removed or renamed; no diagnostic message changed; no raw evidence log deleted; no `src` or `tests`
byte changed. RECORD RULE: records and evidence do not pin sha256 hashes of other round records, review files, REPORTs or
the roadmap; pin only `src`, committed evidence data files and identity.py outputs for candidate documents. Reviewers and
coders never run any `identity.py` or other script under `tests/native/`; use only the workflow `scripts/identity.py`.
Red-first and probe evidence must be behavioural. Hashes come from identity.py, never by hand.

### Acceptance criteria

1. Prober: every listed guard row has a mutant, a raw log, a verdict; survivors are zero or fixed and re-probed.
2. Final-candidate logs exist and show zero FAIL for the checker, 25 of 25 SQL results, and a resolved audit run.
3. RED.md describes the behavioural red truthfully.
4. Status line, section (c), acceptance criteria 1 to 10 post-C01 status, C01-R8 explanation are correct.
5. Only line 520 of the contract differs from the C01 candidate; diagnostics and check names unchanged.

### Agreed verification (gate)

- `identity.py` over `src tests` shows no byte changed (empty-input hash as recorded).
- Link and anchor check over the changed docs, with a negative control.
- Redaction check, one argument per staged file, plus a positive control; the staging list is recorded.
- The repaired checker over the final contract: zero FAIL, count recorded.
- Probes as in work item 1.
- Opus review. On approval, commit R15-A1 plus C01 plus C02 together per orchestration.md: explicit paths, `git commit -s`,
  no co-author trailer. Stage evidence like f78f8eb4 (reports, READMEs, manifests, plan notes, probe notes and the small
  final logs named above); never raw logs, never a file that fails redaction. Then update the roadmap opening and Next
  work (R15-A1 approved; next R15-A2).

The messages of every other check and helper are byte-identical to the C01 checker. The C03 edits are in Checker amendments (C03).

- `CHK-I45-diff-scope`: the untracked C01 review file is now a frozen file, compared byte for byte with its frozen copy.

The 17 new `CHK-C02-*` checks cover the OP2 tag, the one-line contract diff, the record status, section (c), the post-C01 status, the C01-R8 explanation, `red/RED.md`, raw-evidence preservation, the evidence logs, the snapshot baseline, the C02 acceptance status, the owned files, the reviewer control, the checker labels, the C02 red logs, the identity block and the log masking. ## Corrective dispatch V5-02-R15-A1-C03 (coordinator, 2026-11-06)

### Objective

Execute the evidence that C02 planned but did not produce, in strict wave order, and then write the record from it. C02 failed
because of a sequencing error. Its PROBE, PROBE2, GREEN and GREEN2 waves ran their zero-FAIL baseline before the fix waves (OP2 tag,
REC1) had landed, saw the 11 expected-red FAIL lines, and correctly stopped. Those fixes have now landed. The orchestrator re-ran the C02
checker (`<scratchpad>/c02/ex_checks-c02.py`, byte-identical to `green/ex_checks-c02.py` in the C01 evidence folder) in default mode with
`--frozen-dir <scratchpad>/c02/frozen` against the checkout: 111 PASS and 2 FAIL (`CHK-I45-diff-scope`, `CHK-C01-frozen-files`). Both
fail only because the C02 record agent legitimately edited `roadmap.md` after the frozen copy was taken. This round answers findings
C02-R1 to C02-R7 of the [C02 review](V5-02-R15-A1-C02-review.md). No contract text changes. No operator question is open for this
step (remaining-steps-design.md has no R15-A1 evidence item; Q13 to Q17 are settled for the contract already under review).

### Dependencies

The seven candidate documents as they stand (identity in the C02 outcome section), the C02 checker and its scratch frozen directory,
`red/red-c02-run.txt` and `red/red-c02-audit-run.txt` in the C01 evidence folder, and the C02 plan mutant list under
`tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c02-evidence/plan/`. Reuse them. Do not design a new checker and do not add check ids unless a surviving
mutant forces a check repair. Plan revisions are minimal.

### Waves (strict order; each wave needs the previous wave's outputs)

1. FREEZE. Refresh the frozen copies of `roadmap.md` and of the review files the checker freezes (now including
   `V5-02-R15-A1-C02-review.md`). Show that default mode exits 0.
2. PROBE. Run the D81 prober over the final contract in a scratch clone outside the checkout; never mutate the checkout. Cover guard rows
   C5, C24 to C28, C34 to C38, C44 to C47, C49 to C56, C58 to C60, C59b and G1 to G8, plus the C02 audit rows. Write
   `probes/PROBES.md` (with the killed and survived totals), `probes/mutants.tsv` and `probes/raw/<id>.log` under
   `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c01-evidence/`. Each kill names its check id. C55-m1 is killed by I60's row-count pin, not its
   stale-to-ready rule; say so (C02 reviewer). A survivor is fixed in the checker, not the contract, and probed again. Use a runner
   that cannot read a missing command as a kill (require a FAIL count and a killing check name).
3. GREEN. Write `green/checks.txt`, `green/sql-final.txt` (sqlite3, foreign keys on, 25 accept or refuse statements with counts, current
   contract hash), `green/audit-final.txt` (audit mode, zero status, or each failure explained), `green/linkcheck-c02.txt` and
   `green/redaction-c02.txt` (one argument per staged file plus a positive control). Every path in the staging list must exist.
4. REC, last, and only if waves 2 and 3 produced their files. Update this record: real killed and survived counts in the D81
   resolution and the invariant-table probe column; a 'Final results (C03)' section; post-C01 acceptance criteria 1 to 10 with status
   cited only to logs that exist; withdraw nothing that is true and claim nothing that is not (C02-R3); label the C02 plan-tree red wave
   (planar_tests build, 58 ctest runs, checker run from `tests/native`) an irrelevant first attempt (C02-R4); take the red-check
   column for PR-C27, PR-G8 and N8 from `red/red-c02-run.txt` (C02-R5); describe `raw/red-checks` as 72 logs, one per C01 invariant
   row (C02-R6); state which checker messages changed in the C02 amendments (C02-R7).

A coder whose required input is missing stops and reports; it never writes placeholder counts.

### Owned files

Under `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c01-evidence/`: `probes/` (new files and `raw/`), `green/` (the five final logs, refreshed frozen
copies, a repaired checker copy only if a survivor forces it), plus the C01 `README.md` and `red/RED.md` wording (C02-R6) and the C02
plan-tree `red/RED.md` label (C02-R4). Under `docs/vernacular/`: `rounds/V5-02-R15-A1.md` and `roadmap.md`. No `src` or `tests` byte
outside the evidence directories, no candidate document, no review file.

### Acceptance criteria

- Checker default mode exits 0 with zero FAIL on the final candidate; the count is recorded in `green/checks.txt`.
- `PROBES.md`, `mutants.tsv` and one raw log per mutant exist; every guard row has a verdict naming its killing check; survivors are zero or fixed and re-probed.
- The five final logs exist; sql 25 of 25 with the current contract hash; audit exits 0 or each FAIL is explained; every staging-list path exists.
- Redaction passes for every staged file with a positive control; link and anchor check passes.
- The workflow `scripts/identity.py` shows no `src` or `tests` byte changed outside the evidence directories and the seven documents keep their identity.
- Record rule: no record or evidence pins a sha256 of another round record, review file, REPORT or the roadmap; only `src` and committed evidence data files.
- Every sentence of the record that names a file points at a file that exists; findings C02-R1 to C02-R7 each have a closure row.
- No raw evidence log is deleted; no existing check id is removed or renamed; coders and repair coders never mutate checkout files.

under `tests/native/`. Identity comes only from the workflow `identity.py`. The gate is the evidence-round gate above. Review is Opus. On

The red-first run of the unamended checker (exit 1, one FAIL naming the C02 review) is `green/freeze-red-c03.txt`; the amended run (exit 0) is `green/freeze-c03.txt`. No check id was removed or renamed.

- D4a, `CHK-I45-diff-scope`: the untracked C02 review file joins the frozen files and is compared byte for byte with its frozen copy; every other untracked file under `docs/vernacular/rounds/` is still an unowned change. D4a was decided by wave 1 of the C03 dispatch (refresh the frozen copies including the C02 review). It is an exception to the Owned files clause of the dispatch, which lists no checker file.
- D4b, `CHK-C02-evidence-logs` and `CHK-C02-record-status`: the PASS-count clause of the first accepts a `Final results (C03)` heading as well as `Final results (C02)` and still requires the PASS count to equal `green/checks.txt`; the Superseded-note clause of the second accepts a note that cites `Final results (C03)`. D4b is a new engineering choice, needed because the C02 sections that name Final results were superseded by this section; it is an exception to the Owned files clause for the same reason as D4a.
- Survivor repairs of the prober (assertions added, each with one new message): `CHK-I10-history-lookup` (C58-m1: a sentence adding a disposition field to history by request ID), `CHK-I51-header` (C60-m1: the statement that the header narrows no clause of any approved contract) and `CHK-C01-prober-evidence` (C31-m12: every raw log needs its row in mutants.tsv). Each repair was probed again and is KILLED.
- Repair after the final record edit, `CHK-C02-evidence-logs` clause 9 (the record quotes no result of the audit log): lines whose first character is a pipe or whitespace are skipped, so invariant-table rows and indented plan quotes may name the audit log next to the words exit, PASS or FAIL; every other record line is still judged by the 60-character window. The reason is the comment at the repair in the checker. It is a new engineering choice and an exception to the Owned files clause, like D4a and D4b; no message changed. `green/checks.txt` and `green/audit-final.txt` were written again with the repaired checker (both carry its sha256). The N9 mutants ran against the checker before this repair; the repair has no mutant of its own (see 'C03 remaining limits'). Superseded in C04: the skip is removed, see 'Checker amendments (C04)'.
- Messages: no existing message string changed. `green/messages-diff-c03.txt` (Part 2) compares the amended checker with `green/messages-before-c03.txt`: 0 existing messages removed or changed and 3 messages added, the three repair messages above.

## V5-02-R15-A1-C04 outcome

This section is the record of corrective V5-02-R15-A1-C04 (evidence and records only), written by the executor of C04 in the order the [C03 review](V5-02-R15-A1-C03-review.md) asks for: the checker edits and the probes of the edits first, then this record, the C01 README and `red/RED.md`, then the FIN phase that writes the logs, and nothing edited after the FIN phase begins. The contract text was not touched; no `src` or `tests` byte changed (outside the ignored evidence trees); nothing is committed, pushed or merged.

### Final results (C04)

This subsection is written before the FIN phase and names the logs and the expected outcome only. The results are in the logs: each bullet below reads "see <path>". If a log disagrees with the expectation, C04 stops and reports; this record is then not edited to fit.

The expected outcome of every log below is exit 0 and 0 FAIL. The two redaction logs also hold a positive control (a file holding the repository root) that must be refused with a non-zero exit, and the link log holds a negative control that must report broken links; those lines are expected as stated.

The FIN phase runs in this order, from fresh scratch copies of the scripts and of the frozen copies, from the repository root: the default checker run, the link and anchor check, the redaction run over the bulleted staging list, the audit run, the redaction run over every staged file plus every modified document and the audit log, and the final record assertions. The identity block in the logs is the block of `green/identity-c02.txt`, unchanged: the seven documents are byte-identical to the C03 candidate. `scripts/identity.py` does not exist in the repository, so no identity tool was run in C04; the C04 executor compared each per-file row, the tracked-only diff hash and the `src tests` hash with `shasum -a 256` and `git diff --binary HEAD` of the live tree instead, and did not recompute the combined hash row.

- see `green/checks.txt` (default mode of the checker)
- see `green/linkcheck-c02.txt` (link and anchor check over the changed documents)
- see `green/redaction-c02.txt` (one redaction check per bulleted staging path, including `CLAUDE.md`)
- see `green/audit-final.txt` (audit mode of the checker)
- see `green/redaction-final-c04.txt` (one redaction check per staged file and per modified document, the audit log, and a positive control)
- see `green/record-asserts-final-c04.txt` (final phase of the record assertions, C04 copy `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c04-evidence/record_asserts_c04.py`)

The three FIN1 logs of C03 (`green/record-asserts-c03.txt`, `green/reprobe-c03.txt` and `green/identity-compare-c03.txt`) are not rewritten in C04: the identity tool they used is not available and the re-probe is a prober run. They stay staged as C03 evidence of the C03 record state, and they are older than this record by design (see 'C04 remaining limits').

### Checker amendments (C04)

The checker is `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c01-evidence/green/ex_checks-c02.py`; the C03 final checker is the same file without the three edits below (reconstructing it by reverting them gives the sha256 of the C03 record, which the probes use). No check id was removed or renamed; no existing message string changed.

- C03-R2, `staging_paths()` (used by `CHK-C02-evidence-logs`): a bullet line whose whole content is one backticked token without a slash and without glob characters is a staged path too. Before, only tokens containing a slash were read, so `CLAUDE.md` was skipped by the existence clause and by the redaction clause.
- C03-R5, `CHK-C02-evidence-logs` clause 9: the skip of lines that start with a pipe or whitespace is removed. Every record line is judged again by the 60-character window; the record lines that quoted the audit log next to a result word (invariant-table cells and one dispatch bullet) were reworded to describe the same facts without those word pairs.
- Frozen inputs of `CHK-I45-diff-scope`: the untracked C03 review file joins the frozen files, compared byte for byte with its frozen copy, as D4a did for the C02 review; the frozen copy of the roadmap was refreshed because the roadmap was edited after the C03 review. This is needed because the C03 review and the roadmap edit exist now; it is an exception to the Owned files clause of the C04 brief like the two edits above.
- Probes of the edits: `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c04-evidence/probes/PROBES.md` (mutants C4-m1 to C4-m3, each with a control and each run on the new and on the C03 checker).

The record assertion script is `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c04-evidence/record_asserts_c04.py`, a copy of `green/record_asserts_c03.py` with two patches (`tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c04-evidence/record_asserts_c04.diff`). The unmodified C03 script cannot pass its final phase on this record for two reasons that no record or log edit can remove: `ASSERT-C03-paths` requires the C03 outcome heading to be the last `##` heading, but the C03 review outcome section and this section follow it; and `ASSERT-C03-order` requires `green/audit-verify-c02.txt`, `green/redaction-final-c02.txt` (never written) and rewritten FIN1 logs (not rewritable in C04). The first patch lets the two later sections follow the outcome heading; the second replaces the order chain by the chain of 'Final results (C04)'. The pinned constants are unchanged. The unmodified script's final-phase output is kept as `tests/native/v5-02-r15-a1-c04-evidence/probes/record-asserts-c03-unmodified-final-phase.txt`.

### Lost first-attempt probe logs (C03-R4)

The runner of the C03 prober wrote straight into `probes/raw/`, so six first attempts were overwritten by their reruns and no copy exists. They are lost evidence and a carried limit. For each one the reason given by `probes/PROBES.md` for judging the first verdict a defect of the mutant text, not of the check, rests on that prose alone:

- N15-m2: the first attempt altered the hash inside the `sha256(git diff ...)` row instead of the contract file row of `red/red-c02-run.txt`, and survived.
- N9-m1 and N9-m2: the first runs appended the staging path under the first textual mention of the heading (the C02 coordinator sync section) and not under the Staging list subsection, and survived.
- C3-A3c-m1: the same placement defect as N9-m1 and N9-m2.
- C31-m2: survived once because the PROBES.md line of another closing row quoted the deleted id.
- C31-m8: survived once because the probe chose a closing survivor line.

The final verdicts of all six come from the corrected mutants and are in `probes/mutants.tsv`. The three survivors that did keep their first logs are `probes/raw/C31-m12.survived-1.log`, `probes/raw/C58-m1.survived-1.log` and `probes/raw/C60-m1.survived-1.log`. In later rounds the runner writes `<id>.attempt-<n>.log` before it re-runs a mutant.
